# Handoff — where the Figma-clone work stands

First written 2026-10-06 when the first Claude session stopped and the owner moved the work to another Claude account through GitHub (`https://github.com/uaburak/designer`). Updated the same day by the second session, which finished every workstream's partial items and ran the integration round. Read `AGENTS.md`, `docs/architecture.md` and `docs/roadmap.md` first; the contracts in `docs/` decide everything; `docs/research/` holds the verified facts about Figma (with sources).

## Status 2026-10-19 — Phase 6's leftovers: concurrent reparent cycles / orphans, Figma's signals and limits, committed on its branch, not merged

Branch `worktree-agent-a2b6d34b17598bfdd` (from main `08d486e1`). `docs/multiplayer.md` §18.12–§18.13 have the detail. **The tree under concurrent moves** (atlas 19 §3): A moving X into Y while B moves Y into X, or a layer moved / drawn into a frame someone deletes at the same moment, no longer leaves layers on a cycle or without a parent (both vanished from every page before). The copy whose change is later in the room's order — the one still waiting when the other reaches it — repairs it with a change of its own (`src/shared/multiplayer/structure.ts`, `Link.remoteFrame` / `repairLocal` in `src/store/multiplayer/service.ts`, journaled through the new optional hosts `appendCorrection` / `structureAt` of FileStore and memoryStore): its move loses (the layer goes back, its other properties stand), an orphan goes to the nearest existing parent above the deleted one; the editor applies the repair as another session's, so ⌘Z never brings the refused move back; another session's move that would cycle in this engine is applied without the move (`withoutCycles`, TS — **no engine change**, no schema change). **Signals and limits** (atlas 19 §2.3, R13 §4): the room enforces 200 editors (past it a session joins as a viewer, its changes wait, no rollback), 200 cursors (hidden until followed or a place frees up), 500 people (+20 editors), presence ≤ 16 kB and 30 a second; it says Figma's `too-many-connections` / `invalid-permissions` (member removed while the file is open) / `client-too-old` / `message-decode-failure`, and the editor shows each in its key's words, two cases (with / without unsaved changes), Turkish included (`editor/multiplayer/signals.ts`). **Checks:** typecheck (node + web + server), eslint on the touched files, vitest `src/renderer/src src/shared src/store src/main src/viewer` 366 files / 3011 tests, the new harness (three installations, random concurrent draws / moves / deletes with drops, four seeds: copies equal the room's order, no cycle, no orphan, nothing lost — 6 of its 8 cases fail without the repair), server 404 / 405 (the one failure, branches' "merge request's lifecycle" review-id assertion, is intermittent and untouched by this branch; `cpu.test` timing flakes under load), a `wrangler dev` smoke (local D1 through 0029, the real FileRoom; scratch, not committed): cycle and orphan converge with a valid tree, a removed member hears `invalid-permissions` — stopped after. **No migration**; the Worker's next deploy carries the room's limits and signals. **Left:** comments' attachments in shared files (the owner's storage / quota decision), Copy link's per-type toasts, Previously viewed / View profile, Cancel ask to spotlight. **To verify on two machines:** both move one frame into the other at the same moment (one move loses, nothing disappears); one deletes a frame while the other drags a layer into it (the layer lands on the page / parent); remove a member while they have the file open (their toast, the copy stays).

## Status 2026-10-19 — the owner's "Wecra UI" snapshots refused (FRAME textData), fixed, committed on its branch, not merged

`[store] warn: … refused the engine's snapshot … droppedFields: { textData: 1 } … 6291457:7437 (FRAME)`. The FRAME is "FileCard", an instance detached between versions 27631 and 27670 (INSTANCE → FRAME). Detach writes `NodeChange::created` over the live node (a full replace); `ChangeSet::touch` gave that `F_ALL`, so the commit was a CHANGED with every field — `textData {characters: ""}` and the rest of a TEXT's defaults on a frame. The engine holds that value as its default and writes it as absent, so the store's guard (`snapshotLosses`) refused every snapshot and the file's journal only grew. Fixed both ends: a full replace of a live node now commits only `differingFields(live, new)` (`engine/src/scene/ChangeSet.cpp`; components.test "detach stores only what it changes", which also checks a FRAME's real `textData` round-trips through the snapshot), and `snapshotCheck.ts` treats an empty string inside a message as empty (an all-empty TextData is not a loss; a TEXT's characters are). End to end in `kiwiLoad.wasm.test.ts`; the owner's own file (a copy of snapshot 27744) now encodes with 0 losses. engine.wasm rebuilt. Nothing to do on his file: the next snapshot after this lands is adopted. Notes in docs/data.md §5.5.

## Status 2026-10-18 — Phase 6 Multiplayer completed (concurrent editing, presence, the server's leftovers), committed on its branch, not merged

Branch `worktree-agent-ae00e4c22b4e4e5dc` (from main `664aee6a`). `docs/multiplayer.md` §18 has the derived list of what Phase 6 still lacked (19 items, each built, not needed or left with why) and the detail; `docs/roadmap.md` Phase 6 has the status. **Data safety of concurrent editing:** a collaborator's change arriving during a drag or a scrub is no longer dropped (it waits — engine `APPLY_PEER`, the editor's `RemoteApplier`) nor folded into the user's undo step; every undo history yields what another session wrote (⌘Z never puts back a collaborator's newer value); a change committed in the editor but not yet journaled now wins over a remote frame the room ordered before it (`DocumentSource.withoutLocal`); a change over the room's 1.9 MB goes as parts (before, it was refused and resent forever, blocking every later edit); a frame never acknowledged reconnects after 30 s; the backlog after a reconnect goes in journal order, once; changes the room won't take (made a viewer while offline, a field no frame carries) roll the copy back to the room's and **stay in the old copy as a local draft**; Offline sync's savepoint before changes made away are sent; a frame's image that failed to come down is retried in the background. **Presence** to atlas 19's numbers (§18.5). **Server:** versions record their editors (the room's own table and column — no D1 migration), a branch goes by main's link password (the Enterprise leftovers' item), comments keep their rich text, the merge request's lifecycle (outdated verdicts, Withdraw review, Close request). **Checks:** `engine:test` 867 / 867 (6 new peer cases, 2 chat cases), `engine.wasm` rebuilt; server 45 files / 405 tests; vitest on the touched app suites (src/store, src/shared, src/renderer/src/editor/__tests__, src/renderer/src/store, branching, engine) green — three installations converge on the room's order under random concurrent edits with drops (3 seeds); typecheck (node + web + server) and eslint on every touched file clean; a `wrangler dev` smoke (local D1 through 0029, the real FileRoom) 12 / 12, the server stopped after.

**Deploy (owner, at the very end; nothing was deployed):** two new D1 migrations, **`server/migrations/0028_comment_meta.sql`** (`comments.message_meta`) and **`server/migrations/0029_review_seq.sql`** (`branch_reviewers.seq`) — `npm --prefix server run db:migrate` (applies every pending remote migration through 0029; 0026 is Motion 8j's, 0027 the Enterprise leftovers'), then `npm --prefix server run deploy`. **Building on Linux (the cloud session, 2026-10-17):** `npm ci --ignore-scripts` (onnxruntime-node's download trips on the proxy), `npm run typecheck` clean, `npm test` 2681 passed — the 25 failures are the environment's (Node 22 has no `node:sqlite` for the server tests, timing thresholds). The engine's native tests: the preset pins `/usr/bin/clang++`, which on Linux pairs with libstdc++ 13 and refuses `json::Value`'s recursive `vector<pair<string, Value>>`; GCC builds it when `-Wno-error -Wno-pedantic` are appended last (a launcher script, `CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_LAUNCHER`), 724 / 725 pass — the one failure is `trace: limits`' wall-clock budget under ASan on the slow box. One real fix landed: `engine/cmake/ThirdParty.cmake` compiled libunibreak's `eastasianwidthdata.c` twice (standalone and `#include`d by `eastasianwidthdef.c`); GNU ld refused the duplicate `ub_is_op_east_asian`, Apple's never pulled it. No Emscripten here: wasm is not rebuilt in the cloud (`npm run engine:build` on the Mac after engine changes).
## Status 2026-10-16 (night) — background blurs rebuilt for speed (Batch D's first piece), committed, not pushed

The owner's test: B2C "Desktop", the /home frame centred, pinch zoom 2 % ↔ 100 % as a trackpad does (scripts kept outside the repo in `~/.cache/wecra-perf/`: `pinch.mjs`, `probe.mjs`, `shots.mjs` + `cmp.mjs` on an APFS clone of the owner's workspace, `userdata/`). It ran at 68–74 fps with 33–58 ms frames, GPU-bound: removing the page's 328 background blurs alone took a 15 % frame from 41.7 to 8.3 ms. Each blur broke the canvas's render pass and ran 3–4 passes of its own. Now (`43c3208d`, `6dca90f7`, `60f58599`; docs/engine.md §6 "Round 18"): the captures are scheduled in waves (draw commands carry device bounds; what lies clear of the captures is drawn at once), and each wave's backdrops go into one atlas blurred by one pass per level. /home at 15 %: 95 backdrops in 5 captures, 364+ → 62 passes, 41.7 → 8–17 ms; pinch 0.9 s: 96–105 fps in / out, no frame over 33 ms (0.25 s: 103–114 fps). Same pixels: B2C at six cameras 0 px off the previous engine (two runs), engine-shot r7 / r17 / r17b / e4 on WebGPU and WebGL all ok (WebGL needs `SHOT_TIMEOUT=900`: SwiftShader is slow), `engine:test` 738 cases. `engine_stats` gains `passes`, `backdrops`, `captures`.

Also found: `engine-bench` had lost its `@shared/` alias (fixed, `857301c7`); "renderer: rounded clipping frames batch" fails (1200 shapes, not 400) when run inside `-tc=renderer*` but passes alone and in the full suite — older than this round. The first open of the B2C copy after an upgrade takes ~13 s (the store's one-time migration, `sourceOpened` 8 s), later opens 5 s; memory 3.2–3.6 GB at open. Next: the CPU at very low zoom (10 ms a frame at 3 %, `drawContent` with its inlined text drawing the largest part), then memory.

## Status 2026-10-15 (evening) — canvas zoom / pan: the best version so far (owner), pushed

The owner tried it and called it the best version so far — to look at again later. Zoom and pan as built now (`04b80636` and before): Figma's wheel rates (zoom 4^(delta/100), pans in device pixels), no hover during a wheel, every zoom frame drawn sharp at full resolution with text under 6 device px drawn as its lines' ink bars while the zoom goes on (the real glyphs come back when it settles), LOD for heavy pages (effects without an offscreen pass under 48 device px, filled frames / instances under 12 device px drawn as their fill), the scaled cache only past a 250 ms zoom frame. Measured on the owner's B2C copy: Test and Desktop pages zoom with no pixelated frame at ~100–110 fps on a loaded machine. Tried and reverted (worse for the owner, kept in git history): tiles drawn ahead at several zoom levels with zooms compositing them (213b24c4…f607ead7), and the half resolution while zooming (blurred). Next ideas when it's picked up again: a glyph atlas for small text (instead of the ink bars), fewer shape instances on dense pages, first visits while images arrive.

**Live behaviour (`docs/research/figma/live/behaviour/`) overrides the audits where they disagree — check each round-7 branch against it before merging:**
- Esc clears the whole selection (audit selection #9 right); ⇧Enter and \ select the parent. Enter on a frame selects all its children (hidden/locked too); Enter on a shape opens vector edit. Tab/⇧Tab step through siblings in Layers order, wrap, and do NOT skip hidden/locked.
- Clicking a top-level frame's empty background acts like empty canvas and dragging there draws a marquee (ours already does this — audit selection #1's "background drag" part is wrong; frame-title behaviour still to build). A marquee over part of a frame selects its children. A layer dropped onto a frame nests by cursor position even when bigger than the frame (audit selection #6's "don't nest into smaller frames" default is WRONG).
- Number fields: Enter commits and returns to the canvas; first Esc reverts and keeps focus, second returns to the canvas; "+10" typed over a value sets 10 (NOT relative — audit design #1 partly wrong); "2^3" = 8; "Mixed+100" adds per layer; gap "Auto" = space between; "1,2,3,4" in horizontal padding sets left=1 right=2 only.
- Opacity digits combine within ~450–500 ms ("0","5" → 5%; "0" → 100%). ] / [ = front / back; ⌘] / ⌘[ one step. ⌘D repeats the last move offset, same name. N / ⇧N next/previous frame. ⇧⌘O outlines, ⌃P pixel preview. Pixel grid from 300% zoom, menu shortcut ⇧' (audits' ⌘' and 400% are WRONG).
- Layers: auto-layout children listed in flow order (first on top); ⌘R opens "Rename N layers"; chevrons always visible (layers audit #9 WRONG); ⌥-click chevron recursive; ⌥L collapses all; drag across eyes toggles. A page named exactly "---" is a divider.
- Minimize UI: canvas full width with floating pills; selection brings a floating Design panel, hidden again on deselect.
- Resolved on `r7-design-panel`: fields now do what live Figma does — Enter commits and returns to the canvas; the first Esc reverts and keeps focus, the second leaves (docs/editor.md "Round 7, Design panel"). Its panel compare scripts are in `docs/research/figma/live/tools/` (run against `?editor&doc=capture`).
- Not captured (tool limits): paste placement / ⇧⌘R / ⇧⌘V, modifiers held during drags or hovers, ⌥⌘A, duplicate-page naming, smart-selection reorder.

Main at the time of writing: round 6 merged (prototype/video, slots/grid/DTCG, annotations/Dev Mode/Compare changes) — 765 vitest, 379 native, engine:shot 97 (WebGL2) / 99 (WebGPU), editor-shot 204.

## Starting the next session

1. Get the code: `git clone https://github.com/uaburak/designer.git && cd designer && npm install` (Node 24; `.nvmrc`), or `git pull` in an existing clone.
2. Toolchain, only needed to change the C++ (the built release `engine.wasm` is committed, so `npm run dev:demo` works without it). macOS, either way works:
   ```bash
   # without Homebrew
   pip3 install --user uv cmake ninja
   ~/Library/Python/3.9/bin/uv python install 3.12
   git clone https://github.com/emscripten-core/emsdk.git ~/emsdk
   cd ~/emsdk && EMSDK_PYTHON=$(~/Library/Python/3.9/bin/uv python find 3.12) ./emsdk install latest && EMSDK_PYTHON=$(~/Library/Python/3.9/bin/uv python find 3.12) ./emsdk activate latest
   # with Homebrew (used by the second session)
   brew install cmake ninja
   git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/emscripten-core/emsdk.git ~/emsdk
   cd ~/emsdk && EMSDK_PYTHON=/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 ./emsdk install latest && EMSDK_PYTHON=/opt/homebrew/bin/python3 ./emsdk activate latest
   ```
   `engine/tools/build.mjs` finds `~/emsdk` (or `$EMSDK`), cmake/ninja on PATH (or in `~/Library/Python/3.9/bin`) and a Python ≥ 3.10 (`EMSDK_PYTHON`, else `uv python find 3.12`, else `python3`).
3. Verify before changing anything: `git status`, `npm run check`, `npm run engine:test`, `npm run engine:shot` (headless canvas checks). Each workstream keeps a **status section at the top of its own doc** — that section is its authoritative to-do list: `docs/engine-build.md`, `docs/design-system-usage.md`, `docs/data-impl.md`, `docs/editor.md`, `docs/desktop-impl.md`, `docs/home.md`.
4. To drive the built app: `npm run build -- --mode demo`, then `node scripts/drive.mjs …` (it strips `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE`, which some host apps leak into the shell and which stops Electron from launching).

How the sessions worked, worth keeping: parallel agents with **disjoint folder ownership** (each told exactly which folders it may edit, never `git add`/commit), contracts first, every agent ending with `npm run check` (+ `engine:test`) and screenshots of what it built, and the main session verifying each report (tests, screenshots) before starting the next round. Each agent's dev server on its own port (5201–5206), never 5199.

## Owner's decisions (binding)

- A 1:1 clone of the Figma desktop app for personal use (UI3, Figma's file model and wording). Multiplayer later (2026-10-08): finish everything else first, but keep the architecture ready for it (`docs/roadmap.md` Phase 6). Never sold or published; design previews will be shared with developer friends later.
- Engine as Figma's: C++ → Emscripten → WebAssembly, **our own renderer** on WebGL2 (no Skia/CanvasKit, no DOM for design objects), React+TS panels, Electron with one WebContentsView per tab.
- Local-first storage, plus an optional Firebase adapter for a **new** Firebase project (config comes from the owner later; nothing in the repo).
- All legacy site-admin code is deleted (done in the integration round). The site's Firebase data is left untouched. No import of the old projects.
- The owner reviews the app now that the integration round is done (Home on the store + engine editor tabs).

Reference screenshots of the real Figma desktop app (UI3, dark) are not in the repo (they were the owner's chat attachments); `docs/research/visual-diff.md` holds the measurements taken from them (1 CSS px = 1.3228 image px). Ask the owner to re-share screenshots for a pixel pass.

## Integration round — done

Acceptance (verified with `scripts/drive.mjs` on the built demo app): launch → Home (no sign-in) → new design file → draw frames/rects → close and reopen → everything is there; Trash/restore; two files open in separate processes; quitting with a file open restores it. `npm run check` and `npm run engine:test` green.

## Next (as written after the second session; superseded by the status at the top)

`docs/roadmap.md` Phase 3: E3 text (HarfBuzz, fonts from the system), E4 vectors/pen/booleans, E5 paints/effects/images/tiles (gradients and images in the picker); kiwi at the engine boundary; GRID. Then components/variables/styles/libraries (Phase 4); export, developer previews, the Firebase adapter wiring, `.fig` import polish (Phase 5).

## Open items to remember

- Manual checks the automation couldn't do: dragging the window by the tab bar's empty area; the native tab context menu.
- The engine's wasm output is committed in `src/renderer/src/engine/wasm/` (rebuild **release** before committing); 457 KB after round 2.
- The TS↔engine boundary is JSON with kiwi names (interim) until the generated kiwi codecs are wired (`engine/src/scene/CodecJson.*`, `src/renderer/src/engine/codec.ts`). Image hashes don't cross it yet.
- `docs/schema.md` §3.4 says an absent `stackCounterSpacing` means 0; Figma's own file and the golden test show it means "same as `stackSpacing`" — the schema owner should correct the doc.
- In a plain browser the dev store can't read zstd-compressed `.fig` files (Figma's newer files); the desktop store can. A small decoder (e.g. `fzstd`) passed to `createDevStore({ zstdDecompress })` would fix it.
- The `/_blob` and `/_thumb` protocol token isn't checked yet.
- Wording to confirm against Figma: Home's empty states and toasts.

- 2026-10-10: r14-input-resize — (1) keys typed into a text field never reach the canvas. Root cause: on macOS `registerAccelerator: false` is ignored (Linux / Windows only), so the menu bar's plain-key accelerators (N, ⇧A, [, ⇧V, ⌥A…) stayed key equivalents, and a letter a field inserts is a key the page leaves unhandled — Electron handed it to the menu bar (N in the Agents composer → Zoom to next frame, ⇧A → Add auto layout). Main now ignores a `triggeredByAccelerator` click without ⌘ / ⌃ (`runsFromMenuBar`), the editor ignores any menu-bar key while a DOM field has focus (desktop.md §8.3 rule 4; canvas text editing keeps its ⌘ keys), plaintext-only contenteditables count as fields. Tests: fieldKeys.test.ts (every bar accelerator through both gates), fieldKeys.dom.test.ts (every command key, tool letters, digits in textarea / input / contenteditable), editor-shot EDITOR_ONLY=input; checked in the built Electron app with drive.mjs (the menu item's accelerator click dropped, a menu click still runs). (2) The canvas spans the editor and the rail, panels and canvas chrome sit over it (Figma UI3, owner's direction): a panel resize / fold / hide never resizes the canvas. Before, the canvas was the flex middle: each panel drag resized its buffer and the frame was painted blank (WebGL2) or stretched (WebGPU) until the next rAF. Now `.chrome` overlays `.canvasArea`; `[data-canvas-view]` (between the panels) holds rulers / toolbar / cards; `engine_set_viewport_insets` (Viewport::visible, Editor::fitVisible / visibleCentre / visibleWorld) makes zoom to fit / selection / frame, keyboard zoom, paste-in-view and the guide-back-to-ruler test use the visible part; the TS "middle of the view" callers use `viewCentre` / `viewRect` (canvas/viewInsets.ts); a window resize still resizes the canvas, now drawn in the ResizeObserver callback (`Engine.frameNow`), also in presentation and the viewer. An explicit initial view (`?doc=reference`) and an empty page's origin are the visible part's. Cameras saved by older builds were canvas-relative to the old (narrower) canvas: a reopened file may sit ~300 px left once. Green: check 1077 vitest; engine:test 512; editor-shot part 1 414 ok / part 2 125 ok, input (WebGL2 + WebGPU: zero viewport changes and every screencast frame exact during left / right panel drags; window resize exact), overlays9 / grid12 / overlays11 / menus9 / menus10 / menus12 / prototype / paints / components all ok; menus11's "Shape builder and Variable width listed disabled" fails as before (round 12 enabled them; stale check). Release wasm rebuilt.
- 2026-10-10: r14-tabbar-flicker — the bottom toolbar jittered while a panel's width was dragged (owner's report; first thought to be the tab bar, which is untouched). Root cause: the toolbar sat in `[data-canvas-view]` (the part between the panels) at `left: 50%` plus a JS offset back to the window's centre, measured by a ResizeObserver and set with React state — the view resized in the drag frame, the offset caught up a frame later, so the toolbar jumped by half of each step and back (editor-shot before the fix: boxes at x 455.5 / 448 / 463, 3 distinct toolbar pictures over 33 screencast frames). Now it is rendered in the editor's full-window `Overlays` and centred by CSS alone (no offset, no measuring; the vector edit toolbar too). Checked: editor-shot `EDITOR_ONLY=input` (WebGL2 + WebGPU) — one toolbar box on every animation frame of left / right drags, no DOM mutation, one picture in every composited frame, centred on the window 12 over the bottom; the built Electron app (capturePage of the toolbar strip in a loop during both drags: 147 / 230 captures, 1 distinct); menus10 / menus12 ok.

- 2026-10-10: r17-effects-tiles — glass / background blurs banded in vertical strips after a scroll away and back (the owner's 87–88.png), fixed at the root (docs/engine.md §6.9 "Round 17"). Causes: (1) the content cache's pan strips (and edit / image parts) drew a glass or background blur only within the part, reading a backdrop of the part plus, past it, the cache's old pixels — the layer's own earlier drawing — so each strip came out a different brightness; (2) a backdrop copy's pooled target (64 px steps) kept what the pool drew before past the copy, and the blur / refraction read it where the canvas's edge cuts the copy (WebGPU: a fresh draw and the same view reached by a pan differed along the edge); (3) tiles drew such layers reading their atlas neighbours' slots. Fix: the render tree flags layers reading their backdrop (`samplesBackdrop`, `backdropInside` per subtree); `Renderer::growForBackdrops` grows the parts to draw by every such layer they touch — its box and reach, whole, closed over overlaps — and, on a pan, by the ones the edge being left cuts; parts merged by least waste down to 16 (`mergeRegions`); the copy's pooled target cleared first; a tile with such a layer is drawn in a scratch target with a margin (≤ 256 px) and its middle copied into its slot; tile invalidation grows the same way. Checks: `render.cache.test.cpp` (3 cases: pans away and back copy exactly what a fresh draw copies, an edit under the glass's reach draws it whole, a far edit doesn't; the tree's flags; tiles copy the whole backdrop), `SHOT_ONLY=r17` engine-shot (glass ×3, background blur, progressive blur, shadow + layer blur, glass in a faded layer; panned away and back at 100 / 200 / 50 %, across, down, at the edge, plus an edit: 0 px off a fresh draw on WebGL2 and WebGPU — before: 150k px, max 198), `engine-bench --synthetic N --glass`. Open: the e8 "noodle from Next to Details (blue)" shot check fails before and after this branch (prototype round 17's colours).
- 2026-10-10: r17-agent-isolation — several agent chats and the user on one canvas without touching each other (docs/engine.md §9.5 "Actors", roadmap Phase 6 note). Engine: actors — `engine_actor_begin/end` runs an agent's write with its own selection and page (the user's selection, page, view, text / vector / gradient edit, selected connection set aside, their events held back), `E_BUSY` while the user drags or scrubs (the write waits, `engine_idle`); one undo history per actor (`engine_actor_undo/info/forget`); every commit makes the other histories yield the fields it wrote (per property, last writer wins), so ⌘Z takes back only the user's steps and a chat's Undo / Apply only its turn's, each skipping what someone changed since; `DOCUMENT_CHANGED.actor`. TS: `agents/turns.ts` one actor per chat turn (no fold, no "stale" after the user edits), outside MCP clients' calls an actor whose steps go to the user's history; tools never call setCurrentPage / setSelection on the user (onSelection gone; set_current_page sets the turn's page; set_selection applies when the turn ends, only if the user's selection is unchanged); the chat shows "Partly edited since" when its changes were overwritten. Checks: `r17.actors.test.cpp` 7 cases, `agents.wasm.test.ts` (two chats + the user at once; a write waiting for a scrub; set_selection at the end); engine:test, check; release wasm. Open: undo of a frame an agent made removes what the user put in it since; Undo for an outside client's calls is ⌘Z only.

- 2026-10-11 (Phase 7 round 1e, spikes; `server/spikes/` only, never deployed, not wired in): (1) **SAML ACS in a Worker** — `server/spikes/saml/` (own package.json; README has the table): `@node-saml/node-saml` 5.1.0 (xml-crypto 6.3.3, @xmldom/xmldom 0.8.15) runs unchanged under `nodejs_compat` in workerd; a minimal SP (metadata, AuthnRequest redirect, ACS) plus our checks node-saml leaves out (Recipient, Destination, issuer, signed InResponseTo, assertion-id replay, size / DOCTYPE refusal). `npm test` there: fresh throwaway keys, `wrangler dev` (unstable_dev), 29 / 29 — valid responses accepted; XSW1–XSW8, a forged second assertion, unsigned, Response-only signed, untrusted key with embedded cert, edited NameID, DOCTYPE, expired, not yet valid, audience, Recipient, Destination, issuer, unknown / missing InResponseTo, replay all 403; NameID comment injection yields the full signed value. Bundle +150 KiB gzip minified; npm audit 0. **Not tested against a real IdP** (none on this Mac, nothing installed) — round 4a must run Okta / Entra; also D1 for request ids (atomic consume) and assertion ids, IdP-initiated, an algorithm allow-list. samlify rejected (CVE-2025-47949 wrapping, CVE-2026-46490), xmldsigjs too low-level. (2) **REST images** — `server/spikes/rest-images.md`, probe `server/spikes/rest-images-probe/`: the shipped `engine.wasm` (4.6 MB, 1.6 MB gzip) runs headless in workerd and exports SVG / PDF (PNG blank on NullDevice); 1k-node frame SVG 49 ms, 20k SVG 216 ms / PDF 631 ms at 96 MiB Wasm memory, 100k 1.25 s at 239 MiB (over the 128 MB isolate limit). Decision: SVG / PDF rendered on the server by the engine (server build, Workers Paid plan), PNG / JPG by client render-and-cache (a connected editor / viewer / the desktop app's render helper renders and uploads; cached per version), thumbnails only for `thumbnail_url`; no CPU rasterizer (4× exports alone exceed 128 MB; custom shader paints can't run); a Container with headless Chromium + the viewer is the upgrade path if raster without a client is needed.

- 2026-10-11 — **Rounds 1–2 end-to-end fixes** (branch `worktree-agent-a4226c5fb11b5d691`; notes in `docs/data.md` §15.9.1). Run in a headless browser against the local server (`wrangler dev`, DEV_AUTH) and the browser app: Create organization → Create team → New folder → New design file → Create branch… → a rectangle → Review and merge changes… → Merge, twice.
  - **Merge did nothing / failed** (only the branch open in the tab): main's room was never joined there (`setSession({only})`), so `caughtUp(main)` was false and the merge refused with a toast that was easy to miss; after one merge, main's journal tail was a remote frame and `acked >= head` never held. Fixed: `MultiplayerService.visit` + `RemoteBranches.catchUp` (joins, waits ≤ 15 s, then `offline`), `syncState().unsent` (also `versionsRoom`), `BranchService` catches up before `final` and re-checks heads in `roomSeqs`.
  - **"Preview unavailable" on both sides**: React StrictMode's cleanup destroyed the window's `SideDocs` and the window kept it (`useSideDocs`, a generation counter); concurrent previews could leave the window an expired id (`preview` serialized per branch, last load wins in BranchReview / UpdateFromMain).
  - **Spurious "Page 1 — Edited"**: the page's `editInfo` (stamped by the engine on every edit below it). `merge3` treats `editInfo` as an edit stamp: no chunk of its own, never a conflict (later wins), still merged.
  - **Header "Drafts"** for an org file: `Source.location` reads the org folder (`space.projectId` → `projects.get`; a branch: its main's), the header follows `onMetaChanged`. **"Move to project…"** → **"Move to folder…"** (dialog "Move to folder", "Folders"); in an org it lists Drafts and "‹team› / ‹folder›" the person can edit and moves with `projects.moveFiles`. **Plan badge** "Organization" → **"Enterprise"**. **Create branch: Return** submits (`TextInput onExit`; the field stops Enter). **Bursts of /members, /comments, /requests** at open: one each (in-flight + 2 s freshness in `MultiplayerService` and `CommentsService`).
  - **Tests**: `multiplayer/service.test.ts` (visit + linger, `unsent`, members / requests dedupe), `branches/remote.test.ts` (catchUp joins / times out), `merge3.test.ts` (edit stamps ×3), `branching/__tests__/sideDocs.dom.test.ts`, `branching.dom.test.ts` (Return), `memory/branches.test.ts` (two previews at once), `comments/service.test.ts` (one read per burst), `store/__tests__/orgLocation.test.ts` (folder name, a branch's, Move to folder in an org), `orgHome.dom.test.ts` (Enterprise). `npm run check` green (1394 tests).
  - **Seen, not fixed**: the editor's canvas draws black behind the Branch review window while it is open (headless SwiftShader; it repaints on close); Home's team page fetches `/teams/:id/members` and `/requests` twice on open (same pattern, Home side); Move to folder on a branch moves only its local copy (Figma may not offer it on branches — unverified).

Flows: team + folder + design file with 3 components, 3 styles, a 2-mode collection; Publish library with "Allow any member of Acme…"; Admin › Resources › Libraries › Manage this library › Set as approved library; Bob's file: Libraries modal (check mark), Add to file, instances from Assets, a style and a variable applied, a detach; team › View settings › View team's libraries → Design files + Default mode Dark → Alice's new file has the library enabled and the page in Dark at first use; an update published → Bob's "Review library updates" (badge) → Update all; analytics sent, roll-up through `/__scheduled` (the day's rows moved back one day with `wrangler d1 execute`, the cron rolls up yesterday) → Library analytics from Home's context menu and the editor's file name menu, All teams › Libraries; `/me/fonts` over HTTP (member can't upload org fonts, can upload to his own team, not to another; guests get none and a 404 for the file; signed out 401); Extend collection and the 1,000-mode cap in Local variables.
  - **Publish failed with a 500 on the real D1** (the dialog stayed open; the version was half written): `libraryAudience` (server/src/libraries/notices.ts) was one compound SELECT of 8 terms; D1 allows 5 ("too many terms in compound SELECT"), node:sqlite 500, so tests passed. Two queries now. `server/src/testing/sqliteD1.ts` enforces D1's 5-term limit (`checkD1Limits`), so every server / store test runs under it (4 library tests fail without the fix).
  - **No library usage was ever counted** (inserts / detaches never reached the analytics queue, desktop and browser): EditorRoute wrapped the store's source in a Proxy to add `images`; `attachLibraryUsage` registers its recorder under `ed.source` (the Proxy) while the store's source calls `noteLibraryUsage(this, …)` with itself. `editor/sourceImages.ts withImages` now adds `images` to the source itself. Test in `libraryUsage.test.ts`.
  - **Renaming a shared / org file stayed local**: the library was published as "Untitled", and Home's listings, the people it is shared with and Library analytics kept the server's name. `MultiplayerService.renameRemote` (PATCH `/files/:key {name}`; not branches, not viewers; offline logs) called by both stores' `workspace.renameFile`. Test in `multiplayer/service.test.ts`.
  - **Org library thumbnails never showed for consumers** (Assets cards, the library's pages, the Libraries modal): the manifest names thumbnails by SHA-1, the store's blob URLs only read local blobs, and nothing downloaded them. `OrgLibraries.getVersion` fetches a new manifest's missing thumbnails (4 at a time) before answering. Test in `libraries.test.ts`.
  - **`GET /libraries/<own key>` 404 at every refresh** of an unpublished org file (each new component, style, variable: 8–14 requests, a red console line each): `OrgLibraries.getRecord` remembers "not a library" for 30 s (`NOT_PUBLISHED_TTL_MS`), forgotten on a publish from here or an Inbox notice. Test in `libraries.test.ts`.

- 2026-10-12 — **Phase 7 round 7a: Draw engine, model and render** (branch `worktree-agent-a0251707b27230a25`; native only, no wasm build — 7b builds the round's release wasm). As built: `docs/draw.md` §4.10.
  - **Transform groups** (TRANSFORM 48, `geometry/Repeat.*`): repeats evaluated as derived instances (linear / radial, px / relative, order, the 10 000 cap), the group's box their bounds (a layout system write), drawn once per copy with per-copy culling, damage over every copy, hit-testing mapped back to the source layer, booleans over the copies, SVG `<g transform>` / PDF `cm` per copy (not Figma's source-only export).
  - **Dynamic strokes** (`geometry/DynamicStroke.*`): the seeded wandering centre line (our constants: 100 px wavelength at frequency 1, amplitude 0.5 × wiggle × weight), used by the renderer, render bounds, hit-testing and the exporters; brushes now count in render bounds, hit-testing and SVG / PDF too.
  - **Text on a path** (TEXT_PATH 41, `text/TextPathLayout.*`): one shaped line placed and turned along the node's network from `textPathStart` (alignment, flip, overflow), `Glyph.rotation` everywhere glyphs are drawn, outlined, hit or stored (`derivedTextData`), the path drawn as the hover / selection outline, SVG `<tspan rotate>` with Outline text off.
  - **Pattern strokes**: weren't drawn on the canvas at all; now the tiles show through the stroke.
  - Minimal touches outside 7a's folders, all in `engine/src/editor/`: `Editor::Editor` sets the TEXT_PATH layout source; `Editor::textLayout` lays out TEXT_PATH; `markDirty` lays out a TRANSFORM whose modifiers change; the text cache drops on `F_VECTOR_DATA`; `encodeDerivedFields` stores a TEXT_PATH's layout.
  - **Verify**: `npm run engine:test` (`r7.draw.test.cpp`, 16 cases).
  - **For 7b**: the internal APIs are listed in docs/draw.md §4.10 "For 7b".
  - **Open**: SVG / PDF still skip PATTERN paints (fills and strokes); repeated SVG ids in copies; every constant waits for a Draw `.fig` (§11).

- 2026-10-12 — **Figma's account menu and several accounts** (worktree branch; docs/desktop.md §18.7). Home's account menu is Figma's (header with photo, the name underlined → Settings › Account, the email | Change theme › (Light / Dark / Use system setting) and Settings | the accounts when more than one, Add account, Log out, Log out of all accounts), Community left out, the org spaces only on the space row's switcher. Main keeps up to 10 sealed sessions (`userData/account/sessions.json`, the old `session.json` read once), one current; Add account = sign-in while signed in, then a switch; switching settles the files, closes the file tabs (tabs `reset`), pushes the new session to the store and reloads Home; Log out drops the current account only (the next one takes over; the welcome page when none is left); a refused session or the idle timeout drops only that account. The local workspace stays shared across accounts. No server change.
